### Completion record — O-01 (arena/91eff436): **done**
- **Deliverable:** `model/index.html`, a standalone page with these sections: This week, Stale listings, Team ratings,
  Flagged for review, Method & sources. Data files: `model/data/lines.csv|json`, `teams.csv`, `flags.csv`. Method and
  reproduction steps are in `model/README.md`.
- **Coverage (inputs fetched 2026-10-13T18:26:54Z):** 86 CS2 fixtures. That is the union of
  the `/cs2-pickems` and `/lobby` TransferState: 27 dated in the current pick'em week and
  59 stale listings. Our line covers **18/27 this-week** fixtures and 49/86 overall.
  Every blank has a per-team reason (not in VRS, ambiguous same-name VRS rows, or roster mismatch) and a flag.
- **Sources:** Valve Regional Standings (official GitHub, `live/2026/standings_global_2026_10_05.md` @
  `eec4853d7b`); HLTV World Ranking (https://www.hltv.org/ranking/teams/2026/october/12);
  Hotspawn TransferState for fixture identity only (pick % dropped on ingest). Every row links its Valve line
  (`?plain=1#L<n>`), Valve details page, HLTV team page and Hotspawn fixture.
- **Model:** Valve's own expected-win function (fit.js `priorWinExpectation`, rd = 75), re-calibrated on Valve's published
  history with no look-ahead: **k = 0.4568** vs raw 0.972821, fitted on 6765 matches. Held-out
  2026-08-03..2026-10-05: log loss 0.66185 → 0.64351,
  Brier 0.22936 → 0.22575.
- **Verification:** 11 tests pass, locally and in Actions. They cover:
  * the closed form vs Valve's actual `fit.js`/`glicko.js` under Node (12 dp);
  * every published VRS value re-read from the snapshot line it links;
  * the snapshot SHA-256 equal to Valve's file at the pinned commit;
  * no fuzzy name matches;
  * no Hotspawn pick % anywhere in inputs or outputs;
  * the pick depending only on our line;
  * market no-vig recomputed from raw odds;
  * calibration adopted only if it improves out of sample.
  Link check: every internal link and anchor on `model/index.html` returns 200, 12 sampled GitHub source links return 200,
  and HLTV's Envy page (team/5991) was checked by hand: it lists Maka, Lucky, misutaaa, Kursy and Graviti, the same five
  as VRS "3DMAX", and shows Stake Pulse Beat III with HEROIC as first opponent.
- **Flags raised (page section "Flagged for review"; all non-blocking):** 24 team-level flags (18 not in VRS, 5 ambiguous,
  1 roster-matched under a different name: Envy → 3DMAX) and system flags:
  * M-S1: 59 stale Hotspawn listings.
  * M-S2: lobby counts disagree ("60 matches this week" vs 85/86 in the filter vs 27 dated this week).
  * M-S3: no series format.
  * M-S4: VRS date.
  * M-S5/M-S6: calibration.
  * M-S8: market reference timestamps after commit.

At audit time that snapshot was about 1h33m old. The main Pages build [run 38459246083](https://github.com/buffedlizard55-lab/MaltaProj1/actions/runs/38459246083) succeeded and the Pages API reported `built`; that confirms static deployment, not a fresh data collection.

The branch snapshot locally validates as 258 rendered matches, 14 source quote records, 8 calculated and 250 unverified/blank fixture lines, 380 inventory rows, 34 retained evidence items, and 22 findings. `python scripts/validate.py` passed: 11 dashboard pages, 743 internal links, and zero table rows without an official source link.

The generated `docs/verified-lines.html` contains an unescaped literal `<script id='ng-state'>` in visible copy at line 112 and further instances in the F-29 text. The corresponding strings in [`scripts/6d9c321d_build.py`](https://github.com/buffedlizard55-lab/MaltaProj1/blob/main/scripts/6d9c321d_build.py#L176-L181) and its F-29 summary are interpolated as markup: the first script opener causes subsequent content to be parsed as script text, so the audit anchors are not present in the parsed page. This conflicts with the earlier PR #33 note that those anchors were present. No source files were edited.
- Offline verification passed: 37 collector/site/source tests; 22 assurance tests; 13 model tests passed with 1 Valve-JavaScript comparison skipped because `VRS_REPO_DIR` was not supplied; `node --check webui/dashboard.js`; and `git diff --check`. The finding is about missing scheduled executions, not proof of why GitHub did not enqueue them.

## Flagged irregularities (also rendered on docs/flagged.html)
- F-COV: Hotspawn lobby advertises 86 total matches (60 this week, CS2 + LoL). Static scrape captured 26 server-rendered fixtures; the remaining ~60 load client-side via React and are NOT fabricated. LoL tab is also not enumerated.
- F-NOLINE (9 matches: H-10, H-12, H-13, H-16, H-19, H-22, H-26 plus 2 others): no verifiable external odds found at scrape time; probabilities left blank.
- F-DISPUTE (H-01 ex-Keyd/Galorys): Books sharply disagree — Polymarket Galorys 1.47 favorite, vprognoze Galorys ~1.43–1.70, stavka.tv ex-Keyd 1.20 favorite. Used Polymarket; flagged.
- F-DISPUTE (H-03 Gothic/Lavked): Market leans Gothic while Hotspawn public leans Lavked 88%.
- F-DISPUTE (H-06 HOTU/fnatic): Near coinflip at Marathonbet (1.87/1.84); HLTV widget shows 31.6/68.4 fnatic; Hotspawn public 10/90 — crowd overrates fnatic.
- F-DISPUTE (H-09 Legacy/PARIVISION): Polymarket/EGW lean Legacy; HLTV widget leans PARIVISION at capture.
- F-DISPUTE (H-14 MIBR/BC.Game): Books split — HLTV widget strong BC.Game; Polymarket near-even; EGW MIBR favorite. VRS ranks MIBR #13 vs BC.Game #47 favoring MIBR, conflicting with some lines.
- F-DISPUTE (H-15 Heroic/Envy): Polymarket Heroic favorite; HLTV widget Envy favorite.
- F-DISPUTE (H-21 Voca/Phantom): Polymarket Phantom heavy favorite (1.03); HLTV widget 69.2/30.8 Voca.
- F-LIQUIDITY: Several B/C-tier BO1 markets have low volume and possible stale odds (H-02, H-07, H-18, H-24).

## Session `arena/616c2455` — P-02 refresh: external prices for every open lobby row (2026-10-13 UTC)

- **Fixed branch:** `arena/616c2455-maltaproj1` (Arena session constraint; the `session-<id>/<task>` naming in the brief cannot be used here, and no other branch is created or pushed).
- **Claim P-02 — in progress → done (this entry):** replace the one-off per-match prices in `picks/` with one dated reading of the EGamersWorld CS2 matches listing, so every open lobby row has an external line or is left blank and flagged.
- **Exclusive file scope:** `picks/**` (build script, generated pages, `picks/data/egw_listing_2026-10-13.csv`) and this append-only log. No change to root `index.html`, `docs/`, `pickem/`, `data/`, `model/`, workflows, or any other session's files.

### Sources (fetched 2026-10-13 ~21:58–22:05 UTC)
- Hotspawn lobby, all 3 chunks: https://play.hotspawn.com/lobby (fixture list, times in EDT, Locked/Open state; Hotspawn crowd % not used)
- EGamersWorld CS2 matches listing, chunks 0–9 read: https://egamersworld.com/counterstrike/matches (headline odds per row; each row's match link is in `picks/data/egw_listing_2026-10-13.csv`)
- Own line: `model/data/lines.csv` (session 91eff436, Valve Regional Standings model, read-only, not re-run). VRS source: https://github.com/ValveSoftware/counter-strike_regional_standings/blob/eec4853d7bcf0f1f7efe7f0d901234bef7e98b75/live/2026/standings_global_2026_10_05.md

### Verification performed
- Pairing check: for each open row the EGW listing's team names were matched to the Hotspawn pair, and EGW kickoff time (UTC+2 on EGW) was converted and matched to the Hotspawn start time (EDT−4 → UTC). Every priced row matches on both.
- Prices are used only when both sides are shown with distinct odds. 1.80/1.80 pairs and listings with no opponent name are treated as placeholders and left blank (4 rows).
- De-vig: p_A = (1/o_A)/((1/o_A)+(1/o_B)). Overround is stored per row.
- Build: `python3 picks/build.py` → 32 rows, 29 open, 25 with an external line, 22 with an own-model line, 25 picks. Local links in `picks/*.html` checked: 0 broken.

### Results
- 25 of 29 open rows have an external-line pick. 4 open rows are blank (no verified price; placeholders). 1 row (SUKEBAN vs your end) is now Locked and is not pickable.
- Earlier per-match readings are superseded by the listing reading. Changes are listed in the Flagged page as IR-2210-01.

- IR-2210-02 placeholders 1.80/1.80 on four rows.
- IR-2210-03 "WBT" = WROTBERRY identity carried from A-07, not re-verified here.
- IR-2210-04 SUKEBAN row Locked.
- IR-2210-05 single aggregator for every external price.

### Completion record — E-01..E-03 (arena/e3315a54, 2026-10-13 ~21:20 UTC)

- **Fresh source pass:** re-read the official [Hotspawn lobby](https://play.hotspawn.com/lobby) and [CS2 Pick'em page](https://play.hotspawn.com/cs2-pickems) with the current capture window recorded as `2026-10-13T21:20:00Z`. The live lobby showed 90 CS2 filter entries and 30 visible current cards; 5 current cards were lobby-only and absent from `/cs2-pickems`, while two older current-week records were already live/completed and retained for audit. The reconciled current set is 32 records, plus 59 official TransferState rows whose starts are in June/July 2026 and are explicitly stale/not actionable. The generated output therefore has 91 CS2 rows without inventing hidden matches.
- **Fixture verification:** current rows were cross-checked against official [HLTV match records](https://www.hltv.org/matches), including the 5 lobby-only cards: [your end–SUKEBAN](https://www.hltv.org/matches/2399460/your-end-vs-sukeban-cct-2026-south-america-series-7), [Enjoy–B8 Academy](https://www.hltv.org/matches/2399461/enjoy-vs-b8-academy-cct-2026-europe-series-12-closed-qualifier), [Banda Chuya–6666](https://www.hltv.org/matches/2399462/banda-chuya-vs-6666-cct-2026-europe-series-12-closed-qualifier), [PCIFIC–Ryvex](https://www.hltv.org/matches/2399463/pcific-vs-ryvex-cct-2026-europe-series-12-closed-qualifier), and [mellren–Privateer](https://www.hltv.org/matches/2399471/mellren-vs-privateer-cct-2026-europe-series-12-closed-qualifier). Existing current fixture identity uses the verified match records retained in `data/6d9c321d/matches_verified.csv`.
- **External market pass:** read the official [EGamersWorld CS2 listing](https://egamersworld.com/counterstrike/matches) during the same capture window. Confirmed two-way pairs were recorded in `data/e3315a54/current.csv` with source URL and capture timestamp. 1.80/1.80 placeholders, started/live quotes, missing match identity, and time/fixture mismatches remain blank and flagged. The [Pinnacle esports highlights page](https://www.pinnacle.com/en/esports/matchups/highlights/) is retained as a second-source review link where a matching pre-match pair was visible; no Pinnacle credential or login is used.
- **Independent model:** old fixture model inputs are read-only from the published Valve-VRS model ledger, while the five new cards use the official Valve Regional Standings snapshot [2026-10-05](https://github.com/ValveSoftware/counter-strike_regional_standings/blob/eec4853d7bcf0f1f7efe7f0d901234bef7e98b75/live/2026/standings_global_2026_10_05.md) and a deterministic logistic points calculation. Official [HLTV World Ranking 2026-10-12](https://www.hltv.org/ranking/teams/2026/october/12) is linked as a cross-check. Unambiguous VRS ratings yield a model line; ambiguous/unranked teams remain blank.
- **Deliverables:** `scripts/e3315a54_build.py`; `data/e3315a54/current.csv`, `stale.csv`, `flags.csv`, `snapshot.json`; `mobile/index.html`, `all-matches.html`, `s-tier.html`, `b-tier.html`, `c-tier.html`, `flagged.html`, `sources.html`, `methodology.html`, and `style.css`; `tests/test_e3315a54.py`. The mobile dashboard does not display Hotspawn crowd percentages and labels market no-vig values separately from our VRS estimates.
- **Verification:** `python3 scripts/e3315a54_build.py` produced 32 current rows, 59 stale rows, 24 current external market lines, and 28 current own-model lines. `python3 -m unittest tests/test_e3315a54.py -v` passed 8 tests covering coverage, uniqueness, normalized probabilities, pick gates, crowd exclusion, source links, internal links, and flag links. `git diff --check` passed. Existing repository gates also pass: `python3 tests/audit_site.py`, `python3 assurance/test_audit.py`, `python3 model/test_model.py`, `python3 -m unittest discover -s tests` and `python3 scripts/validate.py` (the latter covers the existing dashboard, not the new mobile output).
- **Irregularities published on `mobile/flagged.html`:** `GLOBAL-01` Hotspawn count mismatch (90 CS2 filter / 85 CS2 page / 68 header / 91 TransferState records); `GLOBAL-02` five lobby-only cards with no Hotspawn match ID; `GLOBAL-04` mutable odds and timestamp scope; `GLOBAL-05` 59 stale rows; row-level placeholder, market-vs-model, identity, and started/live flags. None are silently resolved or used to fill a blank.
- **Integration:** root `index.html` received one additive card linking `mobile/`. Existing cards and prior dashboards were not edited or removed. E-01, E-02, E-03 are done; E-04 remains in progress pending the release PR, Pages build, and live deployment verification.

from Valve's own `model/glicko.js` (`Q = ln(10)/400`, `rd = 75`, `g = 0.972821`). Valve's
documented 400-point scale is far too confident on the published (compressed) point column
(it puts Legacy over PARIVISION at 99.7% for a Bo3 the market prices at 54.6%), so the single
scale constant is fitted by cross-entropy on the 23 fixtures that have both a market line and
two rated teams: **S = 1122**, in-sample CE 0.6348, leave-one-out CE 0.6365, market's own
entropy 0.6214, coin flip 0.6931. Stated openly on the Method page: the own line is
market-calibrated, so market-vs-own on priced rows is a consistency check, not an independent
validation. 40/44 slate teams are matched by exact name in Valve's file; `SUKEBAN` and `LFO 10`
have no row and no evidenced alias, so their own lines are **blank** and flagged.
`FORZE Reload vs LFO 10` therefore has **no probability and no pick**.

### Completion record — C-01 (arena/cc8f4dca): **done**

**Deliverable:** `winboard/` — a mobile-first dashboard for the CS2 Pick'em board as it stood at the
snapshot. `winboard/index.html` (all playable fixtures, both teams' win probability, the pick), plus
`winboard/methodology.html`, `winboard/sources.html`, `winboard/flagged.html`, `winboard/assets/{picks.css,picks.js}`
and `winboard/data/{matches.csv,matches.json,fixtures.json,vrs.json,odds_egamersworld.json}`.
Generated by `scripts/vrs_snapshot.py` + `scripts/build_winboard.py`; re-checked by
`scripts/verify_winboard.py` (943 checks, all passing).

**Coverage at the snapshot (2026-10-13, Hotspawn payload 22:19Z, odds read 22:36–22:41Z):**

| | count |
|---|---|
| CS2 fixtures in Hotspawn's server-rendered payload | 90 |
| playable at the snapshot (lock time in the future) | **29** |
| with an external bookmaker line | **25** |
| with our own Valve-VRS line | **23** |
| with a published pick | **28** |
| no line of either kind (blank + flagged, never guessed) | 1 — `1079624` FORZE Reload vs LFO 10 |

**Sources (every one verified by fetching it, HTTP status recorded in `winboard/data/probe.json`):**
1. **Hotspawn** `https://play.hotspawn.com/cs2-pickems` and `/lobby` — HTTP 200 both. The Angular
   SSR `<script id="ng-state">` payload supplies fixture id, both teams, competition, tier,
   `scheduled_start_time` and `lock_time` as ms epochs (so UTC times are the site's own values, no
   timezone guessing). **Pick % is never read as a probability** — asserted by `verify_picks.py`.
2. **EGamersWorld** `https://egamersworld.com/counterstrike/matches` + one match page per fixture —
   aggregated bookmaker moneyline, de-vigged. Verified as a bookmaker consensus, not a single book:
   the WBT vs ENCE match page states "the majority of bookmaker companies believe in WBT victory.
   Odds on them are equal to 1.618". Per-row source URLs are published on every card.
3. **Valve Software — Counter-Strike Regional Standings** (official GitHub, global, dated
   2026-10-05), 380 ranked teams. The file in the repo is byte-identical to the live fetch:
   SHA-256 `df96e7fc…ae61b` recorded both by this session's CI fetch at 22:11:03Z and by
   `scripts/vrs_snapshot.py`, which refuses to publish if they differ.
4. **HLTV world ranking 2026-10-12** — shown per team as a cross-check, not an input to either
   probability (snapshot already in the repo from session 91eff436).

**Model:** published probability = the de-vigged external bookmaker line where one exists, otherwise
our own line. Own line: `P(a) = 1 / (1 + 10^(-K·(pts_a - pts_b)/400))`, `K = 0.4568`, fitted by
maximum likelihood on 6,765 match results Valve publishes in its own standings repository, always
with a pre-match rating snapshot. `verify_picks.py` re-derives the constant and the quoted
out-of-sample log loss / Brier from `model/input/vrs/calibration.json` (session 91eff436) so the
site cannot quote a number the file does not contain.

**Verification performed (line by line, 943 automated checks):**
- every de-vigged probability and overround recomputed from the raw odds in the CSV;
- every own-model probability recomputed from the Valve points on the same row;
- the published probability proven to be exactly the market line when one exists, else the own line;
- the pick proven to be the higher published probability; no pick published without a probability;
- every market row traced back to an entry in the EGamersWorld source file with the identical URL,
  and that entry's status proven to be `priced` (placeholders can never become a line);
- the CS2 rows in `matches.csv` proven to be exactly the CS2 rows of the Hotspawn payload, once each;
- every internal link on all four generated pages proven to resolve;
- every evidence link on every card proven to be an absolute https URL;
- the string `picked_percent` proven absent from the generated page.

**Irregularities found (all published on `winboard/flagged.html`):**

  playable fixtures and its filter counted 85 rows; by 22:19Z the same payload held 90 CS2 rows and

if `verify_picks.py` passes. It is `workflow_dispatch` (not cron) on purpose: the odds source blocks
datacentre IPs, so a scheduled run could refresh fixtures but never prices, and would publish a
re-dated page carrying stale odds. Re-dispatch it when new prices are needed.

The GitHub Pages deploy of PR #56 was confirmed live, then read back and checked line by line.
Three defects were found and fixed. All are on `winboard/` only.

1. **The snapshot line printed "Hotspawn lobby payload fetched unknown".** `winboard_collect.py`
   recorded `url`/`http_status`/`bytes`/`sha256` per page but never a timestamp, while the page
   template read `pages[0]["fetched_utc"]`. Fixed in the collector (writes `fetched_utc` per page
   and at the top level) and in the builder, which falls back to `collect_meta.json`'s
   `run_finished_utc` for the snapshot that predates the field. The page now reads
   "fetched 2026-10-13T22:54:13+00:00", which is the CI run's own finish time — evidence-backed,
   not invented.
2. **The Valve standings link pointed at `blob/main`,** so it could silently resolve to a different
   file than the one whose SHA-256 we verify. Both the builder and the snapshot script now pin the
   commit `eec4853d7bcf0f1f7efe7f0d901234bef7e98b75` — the commit GitHub Actions fetched at
   2026-10-13T22:11:03Z.
3. **Rows linked the whole 380-team file instead of the team.** `vrs_snapshot.py` now records the
   source line number while parsing, and each row carries a `Valve VRS row — <team> (#rank, N pts)`
   deep link of the form `...?plain=1#L<n>`: 120 deep links across the generated pages. Spot-checked
   against the official file: `#L216` = Gothic (rank 211, 705 pts), `#L7` = Legacy (rank 2, 1931),
   `#L14` = Aurora (rank 9, 1716), `#L22` = 9z (rank 17, 1543). These are identical to the anchors
   `arena/616c2455` (P-02) published independently — a genuine cross-confirmation of both.

Verification is now **1,068 checks, all passing**, and includes new assertions that the Valve URL is
pinned to a 40-hex commit, that every emitted line anchor exists in `vrs.json`, and that the
snapshot line can never print "unknown".

#### Live slate (L-01..L-06) — result as published, 2026-10-13 23:15 UTC

Snapshot `2026-10-13T23:11:44Z` (run 38482637767). **90 CS2 fixture records in the payload — 29 open,
59 stale June/July listings (FL-21), 2 locked at collection time.** 29 open rows: **8 with a real outside
bookmaker price** (Pinnacle's own public guest responses the matchups page loads itself, `+165/−234`
converted to decimal and de-vigged, overround shown per row), **27 with our own line** (Elo over Valve's
official Regional Standings file, standings 2026-10-05, 380 teams), **28 with a pick**, **1 blank row**
(FORZE Reload v LFO 10: no readable market and `LFO 10` is not in Valve's file — blank and flagged FL-01/
FL-02, nothing inferred). HLTV World Ranking (2026-10-12) is collected as an independent cross-check
column: 208 rows parsed, 52 of our team names matched (FURIA #4 … B8 Academy #229). A Polymarket
prediction-market reader is wired as a labelled secondary outside source (4 markets readable this run)
but no row needed it, because a bookmaker line existed for each priced row. EGamersWorld answered
HTTP 403 to the runner (FL-22) — its columns stay blank.

Crowd percentages from the board are **not** an input: 490 fixture records carried them, the slate CSV
contains none of those columns and the builder exits non-zero if one ever appears. They are published
once, on `live/coverage.html`, under "audit only, never an input".

Verification: 16/16 unit tests; builder deterministic on a frozen snapshot; 368 internal links and
fragments 0 broken; HTML tag-balance clean on all six pages; per-row source-link rule enforced by the
builder. Line-by-line figures with links: `reviews/459ff624-verification.md`. Arithmetic re-checked by
hand from the published inputs (examples): 9z–Aurora 3.550/1.307 → 26.9 %/73.1 % (overround 4.7 %);
MIBR–BC.Game 1.637/2.230 → 57.7 %/42.3 % (5.9 %); VRS 1543/1716 → 27.0 %/73.0 % own line.

  (which redirects to whatever date HLTV has published) is served. Run 38501253087 therefore parsed **245 ranking teams and
  267 upcoming match links** where earlier runs recorded a refusal. If a board of yours says "HLTV 403, unverifiable", re-test
  with an honest identifiable UA and the undated URL before publishing that sentence; EGamersWorld still refuses both ways.
- 2026-10-14 02:33 UTC: OddsDigger (200, 255 kB) and bo3.gg (200, 13 kB) are reachable from the runner; both are archived
  with a shape report and used for nothing until a parser exists. `oddsdigger: 1 page(s) reachable, biggest table 8 rows` —
  that is the measured result, so the cheap guess that it lists per-match odds did not hold on the index page.

### Contributions that unblocked picks for every match

Six teams had no usable rating and were blocking 5 fixtures. Each was resolved against an
official page (details and evidence links in §4 of `reviews/198cf31e-verification.md`):
`ex-RUSTEC`→Valve #92 (1066), `6666`→Valve #127 (923), `Banda chuya`→Valve #165 (801),
`Envy`→Valve #21 "3DMAX" (1500), `LFO 10`→HLTV Valve ranking #168 (771), `RoundsGG`→Valve #310
"ROUNDS" (548). The first three were duplicate names inside the official Valve file, resolved by
roster against HLTV; this also answers the identity questions left open by `a0bdf924`
(IRR-MF-2, IRR-MF-3) and `63ee81ba` (PF-02) — see §4 of the review.

### Irregularities (non-blocking, all published)

- **Leaked conflict-marker text was rendering as visible copy on the live root page** (two `#`
  lines from the `5545dbb8` rebase). Removed here; flagged in §8 of the review because the same
  pattern may exist elsewhere.
- `FL-DUPLICATE-QUOTE` ×3: two fixtures sharing a price pair from the same book. Checked by hand
  — different source records, kickoffs and team names, so a coarse odds ladder rather than a
  mismatch. Kept and flagged rather than dropped.
- Valve's official standings are 9 days old (newest file is `2026_10_05`); every rating row is
  stamped. The regional files were verified to be a strict subset of the global file.
- Our rating model is weak (RMSE 10.4 pp). It is published with its fit diagnostics, is only ever
  a fallback, and every market/model disagreement is flagged.

Files, all inside the claimed scope: `livelines/**` (board, data, tests), `.github/workflows/livelines-457629c4.yml`,
`tests/test_457629c4.py`, `reviews/457629c4-verification.md`, plus the additive root-`index.html` lines and this section.

**What was built.** One mobile-first board: `livelines/index.html` (executive summary with in-site links to the
picks and the unresolved section), `picks.html` (one card + one row per open fixture: both win probabilities, the
explicit pick, its basis, the source link and the read minute), `unresolved.html` (the brief's separate
Unresolved-picks section: the fixture that cannot be resolved like the featured picks plus the limitations that
blocked it), `flagged.html`, `methodology.html`, `sources.html`, `cadence.html`.

**Coverage at build time (2026-10-14T04:48Z).** 89 fixtures on the slate, **28 open**, 61 locked/past;
**27 with a pick** — 25 from an outside listing (de-vigged two-way; Polymarket, Thunderpick via bo3.gg,
EGamersWorld/bc.game) and 2 from our own line — and **1 published as a lower bound with no pick** (`1079624`
FORZE Reload v LFO 10). 23 of 28 carry a stated series length. Own-line scale fitted to the outside market by
minimum mean absolute error: **K = 877.75 pts, MAE 8.66 pp, n = 25**; the log-loss-optimal alternative (K ≈ 184)
is disclosed on every row that depends on the scale. Hotspawn crowd percentages are never stored or used.

**Sources (all live-read this session, URLs + read times in `livelines/data/live_reads.json`).**
`play.hotspawn.com/lobby` and `/cs2-pickems` (fixture identity, exact UTC lock epochs; page countdowns are
render-time and are not used as evidence — the payload epochs are); `egamersworld.com` CCT Europe #11 and #12
Play-In event pages; `polymarket.com/event/cs2-gothic-lavked-2026-10-14` (50¢/51¢, volume $303.73);
`hltv.org/ranking/teams/2026/october/12` (the 2026-10-13 page is a 404 — the weekly ranking is Monday's);
Valve Regional Standings via the GitHub contents API (`ValveSoftware/counter-strike_regional_standings`,
standings dated **2026-10-05**, blob `2aa28053…`, saved byte-for-byte at `livelines/data/raw/`); this
repository's hourly outside-market feed `feeds/data/market_feed.csv` (read-only).

7 repository checks), including: the de-vig arithmetic is re-derived from the printed prices; the pick is the

## Session `arena/198cf31e` — MERGED, site live (2026-10-14)

`.bs-*` executive-summary hero sits at the very top, above every pre-existing board, and pulls
its four counts live from `briefing/data/coverage.json`. Every other session's board is kept in
full underneath a heading that says they have their own snapshots and are not interchangeable.

### Board: `briefing/` (9 pages + CSV + coverage.json)
`index.html` (exec summary + all 28 picks), `picks.html`, `lines.html`, `own-lines.html`,
`coverage.html`, `sources.html`, `methology`→`methodology.html`, `flagged.html`, `style.css`.
Machine-readable: `briefing/data/{picks,fixtures,flags}.csv`, `briefing/data/coverage.json`.
Line-by-line verification: `reviews/198cf31e-verification.md` (179 lines).

### Schedule
`.github/workflows/briefing-198cf31e.yml`, cron `23 */4 * * *` (every 4 hours), plus on push
to `briefing/**`. Fail-closed: collect → build → `briefing/tests.py` → `briefing/linkcheck.py`
→ gated commit. Nothing publishes if any gate fails.

### Honest caveat now published on the board
`FL-BOOK-RELAYED` is attached to every row whose price came from bo3.gg's public API rather
than from the bookmaker itself (the Thunderpick-priced tier). The flag names the provider, links
the bookmaker's own match page, and states that other boards on this site treat relayed prices
as unverified while this one publishes them because provider and source URL are both named.
This is the reason the counts differ between boards on the same landing page: `executive/`
shows 6 primary picks + 22 unresolved, `briefing/` shows 28 picks. Both are correct under their
own stated rules; the landing page says so.

  `6666` = #127/923, `Banda chuya` = #165/801, `Envy` = ex-3DMAX #21/1500,
  `LFO 10` = HLTV Valve ranking #168/771, `RoundsGG` = Valve "ROUNDS" #310/548.

`briefing/build.py` gained `require_raw()`: a local run without the archived Hotspawn capture
now exits 1 instead of silently overwriting the validated board with an empty one.

(`38512502436`, success) is the real gate for `briefing/**`.

### CI irregularity found while closing out `arena/198cf31e` — TWO RED WORKFLOWS, NEITHER CAUSED BY THIS SESSION

Both are logged here rather than fixed, because neither file belongs to this session
(`tests/landing_43c32386.cjs` is session `43c32386`'s; `executive/data/analysis.json` is
session `5545dbb8`'s).

**(1) `verify.yml` is red on `main` — reproducible, and it will stay red forever.**

- Failing step: `python -m unittest discover -s tests -v`, at
  `tests/landing_43c32386.cjs` line 31:
  `assert.equal(snapshot(data, Date.parse('2026-10-14T10:00:00Z')).active, 0);`
  → `AssertionError: 3 !== 0`.
- Reproduced locally at main commit `eaf528e` (merge of PR #114): `Ran 283 tests … FAILED (failures=1)`.
- **This session's merge is exonerated.** The same test PASSES at `1ce8998`, the commit
  immediately before my merge (`d2a732d`), and my merge touched **0 files under `executive/`**.
- Root cause is a hard-coded clock, not bad data. `snapshot().active` counts rows whose market
  quote is still inside the 6-hour freshness window. The test fixes `now` at
  `2026-10-14T10:00:00Z` and asserts nothing is active. At `1ce8998` the published capture was
  `2026-10-13T19:42:13Z` — 14.3 h before that instant, so nothing was fresh and the assertion
  held. Session `5545dbb8`'s refresh moved the capture to `2026-10-14T04:37:59Z` — 5.4 h before
  it — so 3 rows are now legitimately fresh and the assertion flipped.
- Because `now` is hard-coded to a past instant, this cannot self-heal: it is permanently red.
- Suggested fix, for whoever owns the test: derive `now` from the fixture instead of hard-coding
  it, e.g. `Date.parse(data.capture_utc) + 7 * 3600 * 1000` (just past the freshness window),
  or assert `active === 0` at `Date.parse(data.capture_utc) + 6*3600*1000 + 1`.
- Affected: every PR into `main` will show `Verify evidence dashboard` failing until this is fixed.

**(2) `release-assurance-91e444dd.yml` failed once on PR #115 — NOT reproducible.**

- Run `38512315043`, head sha `6d61201` (this branch). Duration 2m13s, conclusion `failure`.
- Ran all three steps locally at that exact sha: `unittest discover -s tests -p
  test_release_91e444dd.py` → 8 tests OK; `scripts/audit_91e444dd.py` → 217 html pages,
  0 link errors, exit 0; `node --check status/status.js` → OK.
- The GitHub jobs API returns `total_count: 0` for this run, and job logs live on
  `productionresultssa19.blob.core.windows.net`, which is outside this sandbox's network
  allowlist, so the reason cannot be read from here. Treated as infrastructure, not content.

**(3) Note on `gh pr checks` for `briefing/**` work.** PR #115 initially reported no checks at
all. The two workflows above are the ones that fire on `pull_request` for this repo; the board's
own gate is the fail-closed branch run (`38512502436`, success). There are no branch-protection
rules on `main`.

**Result:** merged 2026-10-14T05:00:02Z as commit `83e1c69`.

(`FL-BOOK-RELAYED` 9, `FL-PREDICTION-MARKET` 14, `FL-RATING-AMBIGUOUS` 13, `FL-FORMAT-UNKNOWN` 5,
`FL-MODEL-MARKET-SPLIT` 3, `FL-DUPLICATE-QUOTE` 4) at snapshot 2026-10-14T05:00:14Z, and the
root landing page still leads with the executive summary and pulls 28 / 28 / 17 / 11 live from
`briefing/data/coverage.json`.

`FL-BOOK-RELAYED` fires only when the relayed quote is the one the row actually publishes
(`q["basis"] == "BOOK"`), so it lands on exactly the 9 `BOOK`-basis rows
(1079438, 1080178, 1080179, 1080280, 1080282, 1080284, 1080285, 1080287, 1080290) and on no
Pinnacle or Polymarket row. An earlier version emitted it from inside the bo3.gg join, which put
it on 14 fixtures including five whose published price is direct Pinnacle; that was wrong and was
corrected before merge.

**Also worth knowing for whoever runs the next session here:** rebasing this branch onto a `main`
that has moved several times produced conflicts in the *generated* `briefing/*.html` and
`briefing/data/*.csv`. Those files are rebuilt by CI on every push, so the cheap way to rebase is
to restart the branch from `origin/main` and re-apply only the source changes
(`briefing/build.py` plus an append to `COORDINATION.md`) — a 2-file diff instead of fighting
conflicts in output. Two side effects of force-pushing are expected and harmless: other sessions'
`push`-triggered workflows fire on the large push diff, and `gh pr checks` may show
`action_required` (first-run approval) rather than a conclusion.

### Observation — `tests/test_landing_43c32386.py` now fails on `main` because of `executive/`, not because of this session (2026-10-16T05:05Z)

Verified by running the suite at `origin/main` (`5d34998`) and again at the pre-merge commit
`3b66dd7` in a throwaway worktree — **the failure is identical at both, so it predates PR #160 and is
not caused by this session's board.** It arrived with commit `0b6baa6`
(`executive(5545dbb8): validated refresh from run 38852723650`, capture `2026-10-16T04:51:14Z`):

- `executive/data/summary.json` reports `open_market_basis = 17` while the same capture's
  `executive/data/analysis.json` contains **16** open rows whose `basis_label` starts with `Market ·`
  (31 open fixtures in total, 16 with a pick, 15 with no pick).
- `test_summary_counts_match_captured_open_rows` therefore asserts `16 != 17`, which is what turns
  the `Landing and live-source evidence` check red on `main` for every push. That workflow also runs
  `python executive/collect.py` and `python executive/build.py` live, so the same off-by-one would
  recur on the next refresh until `executive/build.py`'s coverage block is corrected.

Flagged for the `executive/` owner (session `5545dbb8`) rather than edited — `executive/**` is outside
this session's scope, and the fix is a one-line count in their builder, not a data problem.

Separate, non-blocking observation from the same read: the `executive/` board resolves **16 of 31**
open CS2 fixtures, whereas `f323b4a7/` resolves **31 of 31** (every team in the open set is present in
Valve's 2026-10-05 global standings once the repo's verified aliases are applied). The two boards are
independent; the difference is coverage, not a contradiction.

### Follow-up 2 — per-row source links made real, and an explicit `NO_OWN_LINE` flag (2026-10-16T05:10Z)

Two honest gaps were found by reading the **published** page rather than the local build:

1. The Polymarket source link on `picks.html` resolved to the bare `https://polymarket.com/` homepage for
   every rung-B row, because the builder looked for the URL in `row['detail']` while the model writes it to
   `row['polymarket_url']`. Every row now links to its own market, e.g.
   `https://polymarket.com/event/cs2-nip-tl1-2026-10-16`. (Pinnacle's link stays the public matchups page:
   the guest API exposes no per-market permalink.)
2. Three rows rendered "own line /100.0%" because `own_p_a` was `None`. Those are the fixtures where one team
   is absent from Valve's 2026-10-05 global standings — **JOGA DE TERNO** (1081498), **LOS NINOS** (1081502)
   and **Insiders** (1081526). The builder now prints "not computable (see flags)", the model raises a
   `NO_OWN_LINE` flag on exactly those rows, and the method page carries a full flag legend for all six
   flags. The pick for those rows still comes from the Polymarket price; no stand-in rating is invented.

This is the "blanks, not hallucinations" rule applied to our own line as well as to the market data.
